## `AFG-9602-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** UV Lamp Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Hairdressing Scissor Edge Hardness & UV Nail Curing Lamp Irradiance
- **Intro:** UV Lamp Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Hairdressing Scissor Edge Hardness & UV Nail Curing Lamp Irradiance — Work Area Isolation and PPE Verification: Work area ambient temperature must be 23°C ±2°C; relative humidity 50% ±10%. UV-blocking glasses must have optical density ≥4.0 at 365 nm.
- **Key tolerance:** Work area ambient temperature must be 23°C ±2°C; relative humidity 50% ±10%. UV-blocking glasses must have optical density ≥4.0 at 365 nm.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9717:2017`, `ISO 17025:2017`, `IEC 60335-2-27:2012`, `ANSI Z136.1-2014 Safe Use of Lasers`
